# Magma Cube Pet
Categories: Mob Drops, Pets, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Templates with deprecated argument names, Combat pets, Magma
[Magma Cube Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Drop Chance: from Magma Cubes ([[Magma Cube]]) 1%
Mob: Floating Head
Obtained: Rare drop from Magma Cubes ([[Magma Cube]]) and the [[Magma Cube Boss]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: MAGMA_CUBE
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Health: +0.5➡50; Defense: +0.33➡33; Strength: +0.2➡20; Slimy Minions Slime and Magma Cube minions work 0.2➡20% faster while on your island; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Magma Cube Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] dropped by the [[Magma Cube Boss|Magma Boss]] and [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]]. The [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] and [[Rarity|RARE]] variants are a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]], while the [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] are a [[Drop Chance|5Rare]] drop from [[Magma Cube Boss|Magma Boss]] with high damage. The [[Rarity|COMMON]] rarity of this pet is currently unobtainable.
## Obtaining
The Magma Cube Pet has a rare chance to be dropped by [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] or the [[Magma Boss]], depending on its rarity.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|2.5k [[Coins]]||2 Hours|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]||20 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]||5 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|10 Days|

|[[Choco Magma Cube Skin]]|[[Choco Magma Cube Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - March 30, 2024 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[Choco Magma Cube Skin]]|[[Choco Magma Cube Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - March 30, 2024 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Tips
- If a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Magma Cube Pet at level 100 is used with [[Rekindled Ember Armor]], it can be a budget alternative to [[Crimson Armor]].
  - However, this is outclassed by the combination of a [[Blaze Pet]] and [[Frozen Blaze Armor]].

# Megalodon Pet
Aliases: Megalodon
Categories: Mob Drops, Fishing Festival, Pets, Templates with deprecated argument names, Events, Fishing pets

The Megalodon Pet is a [[Fishing]] [[Pets|Pet]] dropped rarely by [[Sharks (disambiguation)|Sharks]], which can be fished up during the [[Fishing Festival]]. It has a 1% drop chance.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Time|
|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10M [[Coins]]|20 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Baby Megalodon Skin]]|[[Baby Megalodon Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - July 8, 2022 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Pink Plushie Megalodon Skin]]|[[Pink Plushie Megalodon Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Valentine 2025 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Trivia
- This pet was not available during the first Fishing Festival.
- 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Dctr leaked a version of the pet several hours before the second Fishing Festival. This version contained the Tooth Collector Ability, which granted a +15% chance to get Shark Teeth during the [[Fishing Festival]], an ability the current Megalodon Pet does not have.
- This pet has the highest [[Sea Creature Chance]] (at 40) and [[Fishing Speed]] (at 80) of any fishing pet when equipped with the [[Hephaestus Souvenir]] and while the Fishing Festival is active.
  - Its [[Fishing Speed]] is tied with the [[Flying Fish Pet]], but the versatility of the latter not being event-locked makes it more useful.
- Its old Enhanced Scales Ability used to refer to "Shark Armor", however, no such armor exists. It was later fixed to refer to [[Shark Scale Armor]].
- The [[Rarity|EPIC]] variant of this pet sells for 500,000 [[Coins]] to [[George]], while the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant sells for 2,500,000 [[Coins]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 6, 2026|Feeding Frenzy now grants +20% chance to catch Fishing Festival Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]), instead of giving 20% more Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]) to Fishing Festival Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Sep 15, 2020|Added Megalodon Pet.|
|Oct 20, 2020|0.9.8|The Megalodon Pet now gives [[Ferocity]], alongside other stats.|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Megalodon Pet was reworked. Added [[Fishing Speed]] to its stats. Enhanced Scales changed to grant the pet double base stats during the [[Fishing Festival]]. Feeding Frenzy changed to grant a higher chance of catching Sharks ([[Sharks (disambiguation)]]) during the [[Fishing Festival]].|
|Mar 18, 2024|Fixed Blood Scent perk being multiplicative instead of additive.|
|Mar 21, 2024|Fixed Feeding Frenzy perk increasing the catch chance of sharks way more than intended.|
|May 26, 2026|0.25|Feeding Frenzy now grants +20% chance to catch Fishing Festival Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]), instead of giving 20% more Weight ([[Sea Creatures]]) to Fishing Festival Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]).|

# Mending
Categories: Mob Drops, Player Statistics, Article stubs
[Mending]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of healing inflicted on others.
Increasing: [[Mender Helmet]], [[Reaper Mask]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 100
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E014
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Beacon ()
Mending is one of the player's [[Stats|combat stats]]. It changes the amount of [[Healing]] received from the player.
## Mechanics
Mending increases the amount of Healing other players receive from the player. Healing received from the player is multiplied by Mending100.
The player's base Mending is 100.
## Increasing Bonus Mending
### Armor
Certain [[Armor]] pieces grant Mending. The [[Armor]] must be equipped to take effect.

### Maximizing Mending
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
- [[Ghoul Pet]] [Lvl 100 + Hephaestus Relic] +37.5
- [[Mender Crown]] [✪✪✪✪✪➎ + [[Sadan's Brooch|Empowered]] + [[Legion|Legion V]]] +162 And +1.4%
- [[Holy Dragon Armor|Holy Dragon Chestplate]] [✪✪✪✪✪➎ + [[Sadan's Brooch|Empowered]] +[[Legion|Legion V]]] +36 And +1.4%
- [[Holy Dragon Armor|Holy Dragon Leggings]] [✪✪✪✪✪➎ + [[Sadan's Brooch|Empowered]] + [[Legion|Legion V]]] +36 And +1.4%
- [[Holy Dragon Armor|Holy Dragon Boots]] [✪✪✪✪✪➎ + [[Sadan's Brooch|Empowered]] + [[Legion|Legion V]]] +36 And +1.4%
- Note that the Legion calculations are done accounting for 4 players due to 5 total being max
- Mayor Jerry is elected +10%
- The player is in Master mode dungeons
- Healer is selected as the class and is class level 50 +25.5
- Renew healer class perk (Class level 50) +100%
- [[Adaptive Belt]] [✪✪✪✪✪➎] +9
- [[Manticore Claw]] +2.5
- Any weapon with [[Chimera|Chimera V]] +25
- [[General's Medallion]] Gives 5% more base stats for dungeon items in dungeons (already accounted for in the calculations [For example: Adaptive belt without this perk would have given 8.75 vitality but because the 5% buff is active 5% of 5 is 0.25 and that's just enough to push it to +9 ] for how much each armor and equipment piece gives [manticore claw isn't affected by this])
- Total additive bonuses: +369.5 (469.5 including base 100)
- Total multiplier: +115.6% (2.156x if converted to multiplicative and includes base 100)
- Final total mending stat: 1012.242 (Will likely show up in game as 1012 )
- Note that the [[Sadan's Brooch|Empowered]] reforge and the [[Mender Crown]]'s "+80 mending and vitality in dungeons" perks make their perks a part of the base stats of the piece of gear meaning the star and medallion scaling applies to them and its already been accounted for
## Trivia
- Players can set their Mending and [[Health Regen]] to 0 using [[Lava Shell Necklace]].
  - Other stat that can be disabled is [[Bonus Pest Chance]] using [[Pesthunter Badge]] and its upgrades.
  - Another example which is different still is [[Treasure Hook]], which nullifies the effect of [[Sea Creature Chance]], [[Double Hook Chance]] and [[Trophy Chance]], [[Tera Shell Necklace]], which negates the effects [[Trophy Chance]] and [[Bronze Hunter Armor]] and its upgrades, which cancel the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]] without affecting the stats themselves.

# Spider Essence
Aliases: Empowered Agility, Empowered agility
Categories: Mob Drops, Pages with UIs
[Spider Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Spider Essence]] Spider Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Spider's Den]].
## Obtaining
[[Spider Essence]] Spider Essence can be be obtained in [[Arachne's Burrow]] and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Spider Essence]] Spider Essence.
- Killing [[Arachne]] in [[Arachne's Burrow]].
- [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret Chests]]
- Trading with [[Spider Tamer]] visitor in [[The Garden]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv58-394]|[[Cellar Spider]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|12%|
|[Lv31-315]|[[Lonely Spider]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|6%|
### Increasing Spider Essence Gain
There are several sources that can be used to increase Spider Essence gained:
|Icon|Name|Type|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Arachne Shard]]|Essence of Arthropods ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Gain +1%–13% more [[Spider Essence]] Spider Essence.|
|[[Komodo Dragon Shard]]|Echo of Essence ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
## Usage
### Star Upgrades
[[Spider Essence]] Spider Essence can be used to upgrade certain items through [[Star Upgrades]].
### Essence Shop
[[Spider Essence]] Spider Essence can be used in the Spider Essence Shop by talking to [[Spider Tamer]] in [[Arachne's Burrow]].
|Perk / Item|Tier Ⅰ|Tier Ⅱ|Tier Ⅲ|Tier Ⅳ|Tier Ⅴ|Tier Ⅵ|Tier Ⅶ|Tier Ⅷ|Tier Ⅸ|Tier Ⅹ|

# Undead Essence
Aliases: Help of the Fairies
Categories: Mob Drops, Pages with UIs
[Undead Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Undead Essence]] Undead Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Catacombs]].
## Obtaining
[[Undead Essence]] Undead Essence can be be obtained in the [[Dungeon Hub]] and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]]
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Undead Essence]] Undead Essence.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv89-220]|[[Crypt Undead]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv89-220]|[[Crypt Undead]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv89-220]|Crypt Undead (Admin) ([[Crypt Undead]])|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv89-220]|Crypt Undead (Admin) ([[Crypt Undead]])|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv91-234]|[[Prince]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv91-234]|[[Prince]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
### Increasing Undead Essence Gain
There are several sources that can be used to increase Undead Essence gained:
|Icon|Name|Type|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Revenant Shard]]|Essence of Unliving ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Gain +1%–13% more [[Undead Essence]] Undead Essence.|
|[[Komodo Dragon Shard]]|Echo of Essence ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
## Usage
### Star Upgrades
[[Undead Essence]] Undead Essence can be used to upgrade certain items through [[Star Upgrades]].
### Essence Shop
[[Undead Essence]] Undead Essence can be used in the Undead Essence Shop by talking to [[Malik]] in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] or [[Dungeon Hub]].

# Airborne
Categories: Mob Types
[Airborne]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Most commonly found flying around.
— Ike

# Animal
Categories: Mob Types
[Animal]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in The Barn.
— Ike

There are many ways to increase stats against Animal enemies.

# Aquatic
Categories: Mob Types
[Aquatic]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Most commonly found when Fishing.
— Ike
Aquatic mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] commonly found in water, or when [[Fishing]]. The [[Impaling]] enchantment specifically increases damage against Aquatic mobs.

## Increasing stats against Aquatic
There are many ways to increase stats against Aquatic enemies.
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|Damage Type|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Impaling]]|[[Impaling]]|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Increases [[Damage]] to Aquatic mobs by +5-30%.|Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)|
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dumpster Diver Shard]]|[[Dumpster Diver Shard]] Animal Resistance ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +4–52 more [[Defense]] against Aquatic mobs.|
|[[Alligator Shard]]|[[Alligator Shard]] Echo of Resistance ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +4–52 more [[Defense]] against Aquatic mobs.|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|[[Tiamat Shard]] Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +4–52 more [[Defense]] against Aquatic mobs.|
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Giant Water Bug Shard]]|[[Giant Water Bug Shard]] Aquatic Fortune ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +0.5-5 [[Magic Find]] on Aquatic mobs.|
|Waterlice Liquor|Waterlice Liquor ([[Year of the Witch]])|Witch Stew ([[Year of the Witch]])|Gain +1 [[Magic Find]] on Aquatic mobs.|

## Increasing Stats Against Arcane
There are many ways to increase stats against Arcane enemies.
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|Damage Type|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Duplico Shard]]|[[Duplico Shard]] Arcane Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Arcane mobs.|Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)|
|[[Caiman Shard]]|[[Caiman Shard]] Echo of Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Arcane mobs.|(same as above)|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|[[Tiamat Shard]] Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Arcane mobs.|(same as above)|

|-|-|-|-|

# Arthropod
Aliases: Arthropods
Categories: Mob Types
[Arthropod]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Spider's Den.
— Ike
Arthropod mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] which is almost entirely represented by spiders, silverfish, and endermites, the former of which are real life Arthropods. The [[Bane of Arthropods]] enchantment and the [[Attributes|Arthropod Ruler]] attribute both specifically increase damage against Arthropod mobs.

# Construct
Categories: Mob Types
[Construct]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically summoned by other enemies - or by players.
— Ike
Construct mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]], often represented by animated objects.

# Cubic (Mob Type)
Aliases: Cubic, Cubic (disambiguation)
Categories: Mob Types
[Cubic (Mob Type)]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Deep Caverns.
— Ike
Cubic mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] represented by mobs cubic in shape. The [[Cubism]] enchantment specifically increases damage against Cubic mobs.

# Humanoid
Categories: Mob Types
[Humanoid]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Crystal Hollows.
— Ike
Humanoid is a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]]. The [[Attributes|Humanoid Ruler]] attribute specifically increases damage against Humanoid mobs.

# Magmatic
Categories: Mob Types
[Magmatic]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Crimson Isle.
— Ike
Magmatic mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]], mostly found in the [[Crimson Isle]]. The [[Pyroclasm]] enchantment and the [[Attributes|Magmatic Ruler]] attribute both specifically increase damage against Magmatic mobs.

# Mob Types
Aliases: Mob Type, Mobtype, Mobtypes
Categories: Mob Types
Mob Types are a system that classifies [[Mob|Mobs]] into several categories that define their characteristics. For example, [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] refers to undead-based [[Mob|Mobs]] like [[Zombie|Zombies]], [[Aquatic]] refers to water-based mobs, and so on.
Several mob types have [[Enchantments]] and [[Attributes]] that grant increased damage against them. Players can speak to [[Ike]] to gain [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] and [[Combat|Combat XP]] for discovering unique mob types.
Once a player has spoken to [[Ike]], they can open his menu with the /mobtypes command.

# Shielded
Aliases: Sheilded
Categories: Mob Types
[Shielded]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type do not take conventional damage. Instead, they must be hit a certain amount of times to kill them.
— Ike
Shielded mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] which, instead of taking regular damage like most other mobs, have very little health and take 1 [[Damage]] per hit: for example, the [[Golden Goblin]], [[Stoneworm]], [[Superprotectron]], [[Star Sentry]] and most mobs found in the [[Spooky Festival]].

# Skeletal
Categories: Mob Types
[Skeletal]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in The Catacombs.
— Ike
Skeletal is a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]]. The [[Smite]] enchantment and the [[Attributes|Skeletal Ruler]] attribute both specifically increase damage against Skeletal mobs.

## Increasing stats against Skeletal
There are many ways to increase stats against Skeletal enemies.
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|Damage Type|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Twilight Dagger]]|[[Twilight Dagger]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|Deal 1.2x [[Damage]] to Skeletal mobs.|Multiplicative (Multiplicative buffs are simple multipliers applied at the end, after flat and additive buffs.)|
|[[Mawdredge Dagger]]|[[Mawdredge Dagger]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|Deal 1.5x [[Damage]] to Skeletal mobs.|(same as above)|
|[[Deathripper Dagger]]|[[Deathripper Dagger]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|Deal 2x [[Damage]] to Skeletal mobs.|(same as above)|
|[[Smite]]|[[Smite]]|Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])|Increases [[Damage]] dealt to Skeletal mobs by +5-50%.|Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)|
|[[Chill Shard]]|[[Chill Shard]] Skeletal Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Skeletal mobs.|Additive|
|[[Caiman Shard]]|[[Caiman Shard]] Echo of Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Skeletal mobs.|Additive|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|[[Tiamat Shard]] Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Skeletal mobs.|Additive|
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wither Skeleton Pet]]|[[Wither Skeleton Pet]]|Pet ([[Pets]])|Stronger Bones: Take 0.1-30% less [[Damage]] from Skeletal mobs.|
|[[Haggard Shard]]|[[Haggard Shard]] Skeletal Resistance ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +4–52 more [[Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|
|[[Alligator Shard]]|[[Alligator Shard]] Echo of Resistance ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +4–52 more [[Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|[[Tiamat Shard]] Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +4–52 more [[Defense]] against [[Animal]] mobs.|
|[[Skeleton Talisman]]|[[Skeleton Talisman]]|Accessory ([[Accessories]])|Reduces the [[Damage]] taken from Skeletal mobs by 5%.|
|Icon|Name|Type|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|Bone Broth|Bone Broth ([[Year of the Witch]])|Witch Stew ([[Year of the Witch]])|Gain +1 [[Magic Find]] on Skeletal mobs.|

# Subterranean
Categories: Mob Types
[Subterranean]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Dwarven Mines.
— Ike
Subterranean mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] found underground and in mines.

# Woodland
Aliases: ⸙ Woodland
Categories: Mob Types
[Woodland]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in Galatea.
— Ike
Woodland mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] which can only be damaged with [[Axes]]. The [[Woodsplitter]] enchantment and the [[Attributes|Woodland Ruler]] attribute both specifically increase damage against Woodland mobs.

# Apex Dragon
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Apex Dragon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 765
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]; [[Ender]]; [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000; [[Defense]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 720,000
[Drops]
XP: +1,147 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 841
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_WITHER_KING_DRAGON_100
The Apex Dragon, also named Withered Dragon, is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the final phase of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Boss Fight
The bottom of Master Mode Floor VII contains a giant throne room. There are five Relics placed next to Dragon statues around the room. There are also five cauldrons in front of the throne near the center of the room. They each have a specifically colored Clay below them. The players must bring and place the Relics with the same corresponding color as the Cauldron's block of clay. By doing so, The Wither King will be summoned on his throne.
Damage cannot be dealt towards The Wither King, and he mostly acts as a spectator to the battle. The Wither King spawns five dragons with special abilities, including the Apex Dragon. All five dragons must be killed near their corresponding colored Dragon statue to destroy them. All Dragons can respawn at any time indefinitely until all dragon statues are destroyed. If all five statues are destroyed, the floor is completed.
## Behavior
The Apex Dragon will attempt to fly into players and knock them into the air, dealing large amounts of damage and [[Knockback]].

Arachne is a [[Mob]] and [[Bosses|BOSS]] spawned in [[Arachne's Sanctuary]], on the [[Spider's Den]]. She has two difficulties depending on her summoning method. Her weaker form has 20,000 [[Health]] , and can be summoned with 4 [[Arachne's Calling|Arachne's Callings]], similar to [[Ender Dragon|dragon fights]]. She also can be summoned with one [[Arachne Crystal]], spawning a stronger version with 100k [[Health]] . Arachne can summon [[Arachne's Brood|Arachne's Broods]], which have 4,000 [[Health]] , or 20,000 [[Health]] . Defeating Lesser and Greater Version of Arachne for the first time rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
## Summoning
Arachne can be summoned in the [[Arachne's Sanctuary]] on the altar in the middle of the room. Placing four [[Arachne's Calling|Arachne's Callings]] will spawn the weaker version, whereas placing one [[Arachne Crystal]] will spawn the stronger version.
After placing the four fragments or the crystal, the spider will spawn after a short delay, and displaying a line of dialogue explaining how much of a mistake the players have made.
## Variants
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

## Behavior
After it spawns, Arachne will run around, damaging players around it. It is recommended to use an [[Aspect of the End]] to escape upon noticing your hindered movement.
The player needs to be at least [[Combat]] level 15 to fight a Tier 2 Arachne.
Arachne has a damage cap of 1,000 per hit. Upon dropping below 50% and 20% [[Health]], Arachne splits into a number of [[Arachne's Brood|Arachne's Broods]] (10 for 50% and 20 for 20%). These spiders, like Arachne, are immune to magic damage and arrows. Once all of the brood are killed Arachne will respawn. Arachne has a chance to respawn as a [[Runecrafting|Runic variant]].
If Arachne has not damaged a player within roughly 6 seconds, she will use Venom Shot on whoever she is currently targeting. This debuff poisons players, lowering them to ~1% of their maximum health every few seconds. A [[Cow Hat]] can nullify the effect. This does not affect shields/absorption (i.e. a [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] Variant's [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Impact]]/[[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]]). To counter this, using Wither Impact/Wither Shield about 1 second after taking a Venom Shot will allow you to regenerate health (i.e. with [[Enchantments|Life Steal]]) while your shield is active. If you do not have access to a variant of the [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]], it is recommended to disable [[Potions|Dodge]]/[[Potions|Agility]] potions to allow Arachne to damage you, which means she won't use Venom Shot. Sometimes Arachne will 'refuse' to hurt you (likely a bug). It should also be noted that the [[Vaccine Talisman]], [[Vaccine Ring]] and [[Vaccine Artifact]] do not protect the person inflicted with the venom shot debuff from getting poisoned. Additionally, the venom effect does not expire on death, meaning on your way back to the boss room you are still at risk of dying to spiders along the way.
If Arachne is not defeated within 10 minutes of spawning, the message "Arachne is bored with your feeble attempt!" appears in the chat and she will then despawn.

## Bugs
- If Arachne is defeated next to a wall, only some of the loot will appear on the ground. The rest will spawn on the next "floor" up. In order to get the rest of the loot, you will have to travel directly upwards. It is most commonly located above ground.
  - Loot from this boss can spawn one or two blocks above the void as well.
## Trivia
- Arachne is a character from Greek and Roman mythology. According to the Ovid's version of the myth; human woman and a talented weaver, Arachne, claimed that her skill was greater than the one of Minerva (Roman version of Athena. Both goddesses have weaving as part of their divine domain.), without acknowledging that her skill, at least partially, came from the goddess. Disguised as an old lady, the goddess warned Arachne not to boast and compare oneself to a god, and that she should ask for the goddess' forgiveness. A weaving competition ensued, with the goddess weaving a tapestry, displaying different myths of gods competing against humans and them being punished for it, while Arachne displayed stories of gods misleading, abusing, tricking and seducing mortals. The goddess was enraged by this depiction and the fact it was better than her own, so she destroyed the work and hit Arachne thrice in the head with a shuttle. Embarrassed by this, Arachne tried to hang herself, but the goddess took pity on her and turned her into a spider, so that she, and her descendants could weave freely.
  - "Arachne" or Αράχνη is also the Ancient Greek word for spider.
- The [[Livid Dagger|Livid Dagger's]] 2x Damage from backstab bypasses the damage cap.
- [[Ferocity]] is not useful against Arachne as it has a damage cap. (It still triggers the animation and sound of [[Ferocity]])
- In each phase, Arachne has chance to be Runic, this doesn't necessarily last the whole fight, as after next Brood phase she again has chance to be runic.
- The [[Arachne Shard]] drop in the boss loot is not affected by [[Hunting Fortune]], however it is also possible to obtain a shard from charming Arachne, which is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

# Arachne's Brood

[[Experience]] Orbs: 37-68
[Metadata]
ID: ARACHNE_BROOD_100; ARACHNE_BROOD_200
Arachne's Brood is a [[Mob]] that spawns in waves by [[Arachne]] when she falls below 50% and 25% of her [[Health]].
## Spawning
Arachne's Brood spawn in waves by [[Arachne]] when she falls below 50% and 25% of her [[Health]]. The tiers spawned corresponds to the tier of [[Arachne]] summoned.
## Behavior
Arachne's Broods will run and jump towards players. Lower level Arachne's Broods have their damage capped at 2,000 [[Health]], while higher level ones are capped at 10,000 [[Health]].

An Arachne's Keeper will spawn at one of 9 possible locations, chosen at random, 1 minute after the last one has been killed. The coordinates for each spawn location is listed below, as well as a screenshot of each location, with a color coded waypoint.

## Tips
- To avoid its attacks, try to attack it when it's against a wall or on an elevated platform (e.g., the mushrooms).
  - Make sure to avoid its webs as much as possible.
- Arachne's Keeper is immune to all projectile and magic damage.
- A [[Cow Hat]] negates the venom inflicted by Arachne's Keepers.
- Putting the spawn locations into a waypoint system can make traveling between them easier.

ID: ASHFANG_200
Ashfang is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that spawns in the [[Ruins of Ashfang]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
- Ashfang spawn 2 minutes after being defeated at [[Coordinates|-485, 137, -1016]].
- They are naturally neutral. Attacking them starts the fight.
## Behavior
- At all times, the Ashfang twins are immobile and exhibit the Scorched Barrier property which nullifies all attacks against them except from [[Y]] Blazing Souls.
- They are naturally neutral. Attacking them starts the fight:
  - Scorched Barrier now deals 40% of Max HP [[Damage]] per second to all players within its area of effect, indicated by a ring of flames.
  - The fight alternates between 2 phases: a Summons phase lasting 50 seconds and a Healing phase lasting about 10 seconds.
Comparison:
|Mob|[[Ashfang]] Ashfang| [[Ashfang Follower]]| [[Ashfang Acolyte]]| [[Ashfang Underling]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Health]]|50,000,000 [[Health]]|2,000,000 [[Health]]|2,000,000 [[Health]]|2,000,000 [[Health]]|
|Vulnerability|Blazing Souls|Gravity Orbs|Melee Damage ([[Damage Calculation]])|Non- Melee Damage ([[Damage Calculation]])|
|Mobility|Immobile.|Follows the player.|If attacked for a few seconds, teleports away from the player.|If attacked for a few seconds, teleports away from the player.|
|Gravity Orb|✔||||
|Cryogenic Blast||✔|||
|Fireball||2,000 [[Damage]] , 1 every 2s|1,000 [[Damage]] , 6 every 2s|1,000 [[Damage]] , 6 every 2s|
|[[Y]] Noxious Ash Cloud|5,000 Wither Dmg ([[Damage Calculation]])||1,000 Wither Dmg ([[Damage Calculation]])|1,000 Wither Dmg ([[Damage Calculation]])|
|Scorched Barrier|✔||||
|Summons|✔||||
|Immense Healing|✔||||
### Summons Phase
This phase begins with Ashfang using Summons, and after 15 seconds, Gravity Orbs.
- Summons: 12 of them are summoned at once in a circle around Ashfang: 4 [[Ashfang Follower|Followers]], 4 [[Ashfang Acolyte|Acolytes]] and 4 [[Ashfang Underling|Underlings]].
- Gravity Orb: 3 of them are summoned over a period of 3 seconds after which they suck up nearby players and Ashfang Followers. They despawn at the end of the phase although luring Ashfang Followers into one causes it to explode after 5 seconds, killing them. Players touching one take 5,000 [[Damage]] every second.
- [[Y]] Noxious Ash Cloud: occasionally lob an [[Obsidian]] block, lingering for 5 seconds before dissipating. Deals [[Damage Calculation|Wither Damage]] every second for 10 seconds, which is refreshed upon re-entering the cloud.
As for the summons themselves:
- Fireball: shot at the player, dealing [[Damage]] on impact.
- Cryogenic Blast: only shot by [[Ashfang Follower|Ashfang Followers]] while following a player, dealing 2,000 [[Damage]] on impact, disabling abilities and slowing them down for 5 seconds.
- Blazing Soul: upon killing any minion, spawns at its death location and despawns after 10 seconds; punching one launches it in the direction the player is facing. Deals a static 2,000,000 [[Damage]] to [[Ashfang]] Ashfang.
### Healing Phase
- Immense Healing : the Ashfang twins absorb the remaining summons, healing 250,000 [[Health]] per. This does not spawn Blazing Souls.

## Tips
- [[Netherrack-Looking Sunshade]] removes the hazard of [[Dive Ghast|Dive Ghasts]] during the night.
- [[Gyrokinetic Wand]] bundles the summons together, easing the lure of [[Ashfang Follower|Ashfang Followers]].
- The combination of Cryogenic Blast and Gravity Orbs is fatal.
- Luring [[Ashfang Follower|Ashfang Followers]] relies on keeping a distance that is small enough to keep them following the player and big enough to not get trapped in a Gravity Orb.
## Trivia
- Blazing Souls use the old texture of [[Burning Eye]].

# Ashfang Acolyte
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Crimson Isle
[Ashfang Acolyte]

[Drops]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 192
[Metadata]
ID: ASHFANG_BLUE_BLAZE_150
An Ashfang Acolyte is a [[Mob]] that spawns while fighting [[Ashfang]] in the [[Ruins of Ashfang]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Four Ashfang Acolytes spawn during [[Ashfang]]'s "Summons Phase". The phase initially occurs when Ashfang is first attacked, as well as repeatedly throughout the fight, each time lasting 50 seconds.
## Behavior
Ashfang Acolytes can only be attacked from [[Damage Calculation|Melee Damage]]. They use various attack methods against nearby players, including the following:
- Fireball: Shoots six fireballs at the player, dealing 1,000 [[Damage]] on impact.
- [[Y]] Noxious Ash Cloud: occasionally lob an [[Obsidian]] block, lingering for 5 seconds before dissipating. This deals 1,000 [[Damage Calculation|Wither Damage]] every second for 10 seconds, which is refreshed upon re-entering the cloud.
- They do not try and follower the player, and therefore stay still.
- If they are attacked for a few seconds, they will teleport away from the player.
After the 50 seconds of the Summons Phase are up, each [[Ashfang]] summon will be re-absorbed, healing Ashfang for 250,000 [[Health]] per summon.

An Ashfang Follower is a [[Mob]] that spawns while fighting [[Ashfang]] in the [[Ruins of Ashfang]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Four Ashfang Followers spawn during [[Ashfang]]'s "Summons Phase". The phase initially occurs when Ashfang is first attacked, as well as repeatedly throughout the fight, each time lasting 50 seconds.
## Behavior
Ashfang Followers can only be killed by entering a Gravity Orb when following the player, three of which spawn from [[Ashfang]] during a 3 second period during the fight. They use various attack methods against nearby players, including the following:
- Cryogenic Blast: Shot while following a player, dealing 2,000 [[Damage]] on impact, disabling abilities and slowing them down for 5 seconds.
- Fireball: Shoots one fireball at the player every 2 seconds, dealing 1,000 [[Damage]] on impact.
- They will repeatedly follower the player. This can be somewhat mitigated with [[Gyrokinetic Wand]], making them more manageable.
After the 50 seconds of the Summons Phase are up, each [[Ashfang]] summon will be re-absorbed, healing Ashfang for 250,000 [[Health]] per summon.

It has a small chance to be spawned when a player with an active [[Revenant Horror]] Tier V Quest kills any [[Zombie]] outside of Dungeons. When this happens, an explosion effect takes place, and the Atoned Champion will spawn.

# Autoboots
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
[Autoboots]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 5
Spawn Location: [[Living Cave]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 155; [[Rift Damage]]: 18
[Drops]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: AUTOBOOTS_5
The Autoboots is a [[Mob]] that can be spawned in the [[Living Cave]] with a [[Living Metal Bootspawn]].
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv5]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Motes]]|500|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|
After an Autoboots is killed, it will drop a pair of floating Enchanted Diamond Boots. Players with [[Boots]] equipped can right-click on it to add the Full Set Bonus: Living Metal Suit to their boots. It will add a percentage of juice to the ability, and once the ability reaches 100%, the armor piece's ability is maxed out.
## Behavior
Autoboots move slowly towards the player that spawned them, removing 18 [[Rift Time]] on contact.
Once an Autoboots is low on [[Health]] it will throw 3x ❈ Defensive Blocks, which are surrounded by particles. The Autoboots is invincible whilst there are Defensive Blocks, and thus the player break them, by standing in the particles and mining them with a [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]], at which point the player may attack the mob again.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Autoboots.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Autoboots.|

# Automaton
Aliases: Automatons
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Automaton]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 67-71
Spawn Location: [[Precursor Remnants]] [[Lost Precursor City]]
Mob Type: [[Subterranean]] [[Construct]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 15,000-20,000; [[Damage]]: 750
[Drops]
Drops: 8–18x Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Superlite Motor]] 0–1x [[FTX 3070]] 0–1x [[Synthetic Heart]] 0–1x [[Control Switch]] 0–1x [[Electron Transmitter]] 0–1x [[Robotron Reflector]]
XP: 100-106 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 73-78
[Metadata]
ID: AUTOMATON_100; AUTOMATON_150
The Automaton is a hostile mob found in the [[Precursor Remnants]] and [[Lost Precursor City]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. They can drop [[Professor Robot|Automaton Parts]], which can be used to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]] when given to [[Professor Robot]].
## Spawning
Lower level Automatons spawn within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. Their spawn rates are increased within structures including the [[Lost Precursor City]]. They spawn in constant spots, and can only spawn when a player walks near their specific spawn radius.
Higher level Automatons only spawn within the [[Lost Precursor City]].
## Variants
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv67]|[[Precursor Remnants]]|15,000|750|100|73|22|AUTOMATON_100|
|[Lv71]|[[Lost Precursor City]]|20,000|750|106|78|23|AUTOMATON_150|
## Behavior
The Automaton attacks with red lasers from their eyes. This laser can pass through walls and deals moderate damage.
